<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> ActionSwapText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">animation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "blur"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> ActionSwapTextProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // Cascade needs plain text to split; anything else rolls as one piece.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (animation </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "cascade"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> typeof</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> children </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "string"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
